        for(var i = 0; i < myOpts.length; i++){
            if((window.location.pathname.split("/")[2] == myOpts[i].value) || (getCookie('CK_LANG_CODE') == myOpts[i].value)){
                myOpts[i].setAttribute("selected", "selected");
            }
        }
    }
</script>

window.utilities = {
  extractURLParams: function (params) {
    var params = params.split('&');
    var extractedParams = {};
    for (i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
      var param = params[i].split('=');
      extractedParams[param[0]] = param[1];
    }
    return extractedParams;
  },
  extractValue: function (object, variable) {
    if (typeof object !== 'undefined') {
      var keys = variable.split('.');
      if (keys.length > 1 && object.hasOwnProperty(keys[0])) {
        return window.utilities.extractValue(object[keys[0]], keys.splice(1).join('.'));
      } else if (object.hasOwnProperty(keys[0])) {
        return object[keys[0]];
      } else {
        return undefined;
      }
    }
  },
  oldCookies: document.cookie,
  cookiesChange: function () {
    return (window.utilities.oldCookies !== document.cookie);
  },
  jsonifyCookies: function (cookies) {
    var jsonCookies = {}, cookiesArray = cookies.split(';');
    for (var i = 0; i < cookiesArray.length; i++) {
      var parts = cookiesArray[i].split('=');
      jsonCookies[parts[0].trim()] = decodeURIComponent(parts[1]);
    }
    return jsonCookies;
  },
  cookieStatus: function () {
    var cookieEvent = new CustomEvent("cookieStatus", {
      bubbles: true,
      detail: {
        status: window.utilities.cookiesChange(),
        change: window.utilities.cookiesChange() ? window.utilities.jsonDiff(utilities.jsonifyCookies(window.utilities.oldCookies), utilities.jsonifyCookies(document.cookie)) : {},
      }
    });
    window.utilities.oldCookies = document.cookie;
    document.dispatchEvent(cookieEvent);
  },
  jsonDiff: function (j1, j2) {
    var diff = {};
    for (var i in j2) {
      if (!j1.hasOwnProperty(i) || (j2[i] !== j1[i])) {
        diff[i] = j2[i];
      }
    }
    return diff;
  },
  debug: {eventName: "CustomEvent"},
  consoleDebug: function () {
    if (eval(localStorage['com.adobe.reactor.debug'])) {
      console.group(window.utilities.debug.eventName);
      delete window.utilities.debug.eventName;
      console.dir(window.utilities.debug);
      console.groupEnd();
    }
  },
  dispatchEvent: function (element, eventName, args) {
    var evt = new CustomEvent(eventName, {detail: (args||{})})
    element.dispatchEvent(evt);
  },
  loadScript: function (url, async, defer, eventName) {
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.type = "text/javascript";
    s.async = async;
    s.defer = defer;
    s.src = url;
    s.onload = s.onreadystatechange = function () {
      if (!s.readyState || /loaded|complete/.test(s.readyState)) {
        window.utilities.dispatchEvent(document, eventName);
      }
    }
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  },
  sessionId: _satellite.cookie.get('WCS_JSESSIONID'),
  createWorker: function (s) {
    if(_satellite.getVar('is_IE10')){
      var worker = {
        onmessage: function (e) {
          eval(e.data.response)
        },
        postMessage: function (obj) {
          window.utilities.promisedRequest(obj, this.onmessage, this.onmessage, true)
        },
        terminate: function () {}
      }
      worker.postMessage.bind(worker);
      return worker;
    }
    var URL = window.URL || window.webkitURL,
      Blob = window.Blob, Worker = window.Worker;
    if (!URL || !Blob || !Worker || !s) {
      return null;
    }
    return new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([s])));
  },
  promisedRequest: function promisedRequest(obj, resolve, reject, worker) {
    worker = worker || false;
    var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
    xhr.open(obj.method || "GET", obj.url, true);
    if (obj.headers) {
      for (i = 0; i < Object.keys(obj.headers).length; i++) {
        xhr.setRequestHeader(Object.keys(obj.headers)[i], obj.headers[Object.keys(obj.headers)[i]]);
      }
    }
    xhr.onreadystatechange = function () {
      if (xhr.readyState === xhr.DONE) {
        (worker === true) ? resolve({data:{code: 200, response: xhr.response}}) : resolve({code: 200, response: xhr.response});
      } else if (xhr.status < 200 && xhr.status > 300) {
        (worker === true) ? reject({data:{code: xhr.status, response: xhr.statusText}}) : reject({code: xhr.status, response: xhr.statusText});
      }
    }
    xhr.onerror = reject({code: xhr.status, response: xhr.statusText});
    xhr.send(obj.body);
  }
}
